# List of busiest airports by cargo traffic

List of busiest airports by cargo traffic

Note for consistency, the [Airports Council International](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Airports_Council_International "Airports Council International")'s (ACI) annual figures are always used in the final presentation and ranking, even where figures given by airport operators or authorities differ.

The **world's thirty busiest airports by cargo traffic** for various periods (data provided by [Airports Council International](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Airports_Council_International "Airports Council International")). Numbers listed refer to loaded and unloaded freight in [tonnes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tonnes "Tonnes"), including transit freight.
